{"type":"item.started","item":{"id":"item_6","type":"command_execution","command":"/bin/bash -lc \"python - <<'PY'\nimport numpy as np\nfrom pycbc import frame\nfrom pycbc.filter import matched_filter\nfrom pycbc.psd import welch, interpolate, inverse_spectrum_truncation\nfrom pycbc.waveform import get_fd_waveform\n\nf='/root/data/PyCBC_T2_2.gwf'\nchannel='H1:TEST-STRAIN'\nstrain = frame.read_frame(f, channel)\n# Conditioning: highpass, taper, crop edges\nstrain = strain.highpass_fir(15, 8)\n# remove edge effects from highpass\nstrain = strain.crop(2, 2)\n\n# PSD estimation\nsample_rate = int(strain.sample_rate)\nseg_len = 4 * sample_rate\nseg_stride = seg_len // 2\npsd = welch(strain, seg_len=seg_len, seg_stride=seg_stride)\npsd = interpolate(psd, strain.delta_f)\npsd = inverse_spectrum_truncation(psd, int(4 * sample_rate), low_frequency_cutoff=20)\n\nresults = []\n\napproximants = [\\\"SEOBNRv4_opt\\\", \\\"IMRPhenomD\\\", \\\"TaylorT4\\\"]\n\nfor app in approximants:\n    best = None\n    for m1 in range(10, 41):\n        for m2 in range(10, 41):\n            delta_f = 1.0 / strain.duration\n            try:\n                hp, _ = get_fd_waveform(approximant=app,\n                                        mass1=m1, mass2=m2,\n                                        delta_f=delta_f,\n                                        f_lower=20)\n            except Exception:\n                continue\n            # Resize template to match data length\n            hp.resize(len(strain)//2 + 1)\n            snr = matched_filter(hp, strain, psd=psd, low_frequency_cutoff=20)\n            # remove wrap-around/edge effects\n            snr = snr.crop(4, 4)\n            max_snr = np.abs(snr).max()\n            if best is None or max_snr > best[0]:\n                best = (max_snr, m1 + m2)\n    if best is None:\n        results.append((app, float('nan'), float('nan')))\n    else:\n        results.append((app, float(best[0]), float(best[1])))\n\n# Write CSV\nout='/root/detection_results.csv'\nwith open(out, 'w') as f:\n    f.write('approximant,snr,total_mass\\\\n')\n    for app, snr, tm in results:\n        f.write(f\\\"{app},{snr:.6f},{tm:.1f}\\\\n\\\")\n\nprint('Wrote', out)\nfor r in results:\n    print(r)\nPY\"","aggregated_output":"","exit_code":null,"status":"in_progress"}}

{"type":"item.started","item":{"id":"item_37","type":"command_execution","command":"/bin/bash -lc \"python - <<'PY'\nimport numpy as np\nfrom pycbc import frame\nfrom pycbc.filter import matched_filter\nfrom pycbc.psd import welch, interpolate, inverse_spectrum_truncation\nfrom pycbc.waveform import get_fd_waveform\n\nf='/root/data/PyCBC_T2_2.gwf'\nchannel='H1:TEST-STRAIN'\nstrain = frame.read_frame(f, channel)\n# Conditioning: highpass and crop edges\nstrain = strain.highpass_fir(15, 8)\nstrain = strain.crop(2, 2)\n\n# Downsample for speed if needed\nif strain.sample_rate > 2048:\n    strain = strain.resample_to_delta_t(1.0/2048)\n\nsample_rate = int(strain.sample_rate)\n\n# PSD from full conditioned data\nseg_len = 4 * sample_rate\nseg_stride = seg_len // 2\npsd_full = welch(strain, seg_len=seg_len, seg_stride=seg_stride)\npsd_full = interpolate(psd_full, strain.delta_f)\npsd_full = inverse_spectrum_truncation(psd_full, int(4 * sample_rate), low_frequency_cutoff=20)\n\n# Coarse search with a single template to locate the event time\ncoarse_delta_f = 1.0 / strain.duration\nhp, _ = get_fd_waveform(approximant='IMRPhenomD', mass1=30, mass2=30, delta_f=coarse_delta_f, f_lower=20)\nhp.resize(len(strain)//2 + 1)\ncoarse_snr = matched_filter(hp, strain, psd=psd_full, low_frequency_cutoff=20)\ncoarse_snr = coarse_snr.crop(4, 4)\ncoarse_idx = np.argmax(np.abs(coarse_snr))\ncoarse_time = coarse_snr.sample_times[coarse_idx]\n\n# Focus on a short window around the loudest event\nstart = max(strain.start_time, coarse_time - 4)\nend = min(strain.end_time, coarse_time + 4)\nstrain_win = strain.time_slice(start, end)\n\n# Recompute data frequency series for window\nwin_freq = strain_win.to_frequencyseries()\npsd = interpolate(psd_full, win_freq.delta_f)\npsd.resize(len(win_freq))\npsd = inverse_spectrum_truncation(psd, int(4 * sample_rate), low_frequency_cutoff=20)\n\nresults = []\napproximants = [\\\"SEOBNRv4_opt\\\", \\\"IMRPhenomD\\\", \\\"TaylorT4\\\"]\n\nfor app in approximants:\n    best_snr = None\n    best_tm = None\n    delta_f = win_freq.delta_f\n    for m1 in range(10, 41):\n        for m2 in range(10, 41):\n            try:\n                hp, _ = get_fd_waveform(approximant=app,\n                                        mass1=m1, mass2=m2,\n                                        delta_f=delta_f,\n                                        f_lower=20)\n            except Exception:\n                continue\n            hp.resize(len(win_freq))\n            snr = matched_filter(hp, win_freq, psd=psd, low_frequency_cutoff=20)\n            snr = snr.crop(2, 2)\n            max_snr = np.abs(snr).max()\n            if best_snr is None or max_snr > best_snr:\n                best_snr = float(max_snr)\n                best_tm = float(m1 + m2)\n    if best_snr is None:\n        results.append((app, float('nan'), float('nan')))\n    else:\n        results.append((app, best_snr, best_tm))\n    print(app, best_snr, best_tm)\n\nout='/root/detection_results.csv'\nwith open(out, 'w') as f:\n    f.write('approximant,snr,total_mass\\\\n')\n    for app, snr, tm in results:\n        f.write(f\\\"{app},{snr:.6f},{tm:.1f}\\\\n\\\")\nprint('Wrote', out)\nPY\"","aggregated_output":"","exit_code":null,"status":"in_progress"}}
